Abstract: Scientific images are the core elements of presenting experimental conclusions, elaborating system architecture, and supporting comparative arguments in scientific papers. However, existing image quality assessment (IQA) methods are predominantly designed for natural photographs or AI-generated content, which cannot be directly applied to scientific papers. The few existing studies on scholarly charts remain confined to visual-surface comparisons, failing to verify caption alignment, citation relevance, or visual misleadingness. To address this, we propose SciFigQual-Bench, a full-text contextual benchmark that evaluates scientific images across five dimensions (clarity, layout, caption fit, context relevance, and misleading risk). The data covers top computer-science conferences from 2020 to 2025; 6,308 images were independently scored by multiple domain experts in five dimensions and aggregated into gold-standard annotations. Unlike previous scientific figure benchmarks, our dataset binds each image to its caption, citing sentence, and manuscript context. To enable automated evaluation on this benchmark, we designed a staged cross-modal evaluation framework SFQ-Agent to achieve auditable and refined scoring through the collection and fusion of modal evidence. Multiple mainstream large models were evaluated on the test subset eval1200, and SFQ-Agent (F3) equipped with GPT-5.6-Sol achieved the lowest overall average absolute error (0.418) and the highest consistency rate (93.4%), consistently outperforming both direct evaluation and auxiliary (Sidecar) visual language model evaluation schemes.

</details>


### [71] [ICDAR 2026 Competition on Information Extraction from Atomic Layer Deposition/Etching (ALD/E) Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26848)
*Fahad Ahmed,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Sci-ImageMiner 基准数据集和竞赛，旨在推动多模态AI对科学图表理解与推理的能力，发现现有模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表理解需要跨视觉感知和领域推理的结合，现有数据集和竞赛覆盖不足，缺乏综合性的专家标注高质量基准。

Method: 构建了 Sci-ImageMiner 基准数据集，包含专家标注的四个端到端互补任务（分类、摘要、数据提取、视觉问答），并举办了社区驱动的竞赛（2026年1月9日至4月8日，68名活跃参与者，1263次提交）。

Result: SOTA多模态模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理（特别是视觉问答）方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: Sci-ImageMiner 为科学图表理解与推理研究提供了严格的评估平台，揭示了当前模型的局限性，指明了领域感知多模态AI系统改进的方向。

Abstract: Scientific figure comprehension and reasoning using multimodal AI requires integrating visual perception with domain-specific reasoning to extract meaningful knowledge, often not presented in the text of a research publication. The Sci-ImageMiner benchmark dataset, accompanied by a community-driven competition, raises the bar over prior scientific competitions by curating a comprehensive, expert-annotated dataset across four end-to-end complementary tasks. The competition attracted 68 active participants and 1,263 public/private submissions from 9th January 2026 to 8th April 2026. Our results show that state-of-the-art multimodal models perform well on classification and summarization tasks but struggle with data extraction and scientific reasoning, particularly in visual question-answering. These findings reveal key limitations and highlight challenges and opportunities for improving domain-aware multimodal AI systems. Overall, the Sci-ImageMiner benchmark and competition establish a rigorous platform for advancing research in scientific figure comprehension and reasoning and demonstrate the potential of state-of-the-art approaches for a challenging and complex research area.

</details>


### [72] [SCALPEL: Semantic Cross-modal Alignment via LLM-Powered Encoder Learning for Medical Vision-Language Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26885)
*Yunzhan Fu,Enyu Bao,Xiangyu Shen,Yihao Wu,Chunbo Jiang,Fangli Guan,Liqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCALPEL 提出了一种语义跨模态对齐框架，通过LLM编码器学习解决医学视觉-语言预训练中的上下文受限、表示坍缩、内存开销大和医学幻觉等问题，在多个医学影像基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLP框架受限于轻量文本编码器的短上下文窗口和浅层表示能力，难以处理冗长、术语密集的临床报告。虽然引入医学大语言模型可提供更强推理能力，但面临三大瓶颈：生成式LLM在对比学习下的各向异性表示坍缩、端到端联合训练的高内存开销、以及忽略解剖侧向性和否定修饰词的对比损失导致的医学幻觉。

Method: 提出SCALPEL框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 临床报告对比微调，将生成式LLM转换为各向同性编码器；2) 非对称对齐策略，利用离线特征缓存实现高效训练；3) 解剖-否定感知目标函数，显式惩罚涉及侧向性混淆或假否定的图文错配。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert和IU X-Ray基准上，SCALPEL在跨模态检索、零样本疾病分类和医学视觉问答任务中均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SCALPEL通过LLM驱动的编码器学习有效解决了医学VLP中的关键瓶颈，为处理临床报告中的细粒度语义信息提供了新范式，验证了将医学LLM与对比学习相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training (VLP) serves as a cornerstone for medical multimodal representation learning. However, existing medical VLP frameworks are often constrained by the limited context windows and shallow representational capacities of lightweight text encoders when processing lengthy, terminology-dense clinical reports. While integrating medical large language models (LLMs) offers unprecedented clinical reasoning capabilities, it introduces three major bottlenecks: (i) the anisotropic representational collapse of generative LLMs under standard contrastive objectives, (ii) the prohibitive memory overhead of joint end-to-end training with large batch sizes, and (iii) the medical hallucinations induced by vanilla contrastive losses that ignore fine-grained anatomical laterality and negation modifiers. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{SCALPEL}, a \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{A}lignment framework via \textbf{L}LM-\textbf{P}owered \textbf{E}ncoder \textbf{L}earning. First, Clinical Report Contrastive fine-tuning converts a generative LLM into an isotropic encoder via domain-specific clinical text adaptation. Second, an asymmetric alignment strategy leverages offline feature caching to enable efficient training. Critically, we formulate an Anatomy-Negation Aware Objective that explicitly penalizes mismatched image-text pairs involving laterality confusion or false negations. Extensive experiments across MIMIC-CXR, CheXpert, and IU X-Ray benchmarks demonstrate that SCALPEL achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-modal retrieval, zero-shot disease classification and medical visual question answering.

</details>


### [73] [Anatomy Contextualized Adaption of CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27154)
*Roshan Kenia,Stephanie L McNamara,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACA是一种轻量级框架，通过冻结的CT基础模型，利用解剖级嵌入和跨解剖关系建模，实现高效的解剖级视觉-语言对齐，在零样本分类任务上超越现有方法且训练时间不足1小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT视觉-语言基础模型要么使用整体体积表示而稀释了细粒度解剖信号，要么采用细粒度预训练但丢弃了全局上下文且计算昂贵。作者希望实现一种既能保留全局上下文又能进行解剖级对齐的轻量级方法。

Method: ACA使用TotalSegmentator将CT体积分解为解剖级嵌入，通过跨解剖关系transformer进行细化，并与从放射学报告中提取的每个解剖和扫描级文本进行对齐。整个框架基于冻结的CT基础模型，仅需不到一小时的训练。

Result: 在Merlin和CT-RATE数据集上，ACA在零样本发现分类任务中一致优于冻结的基础模型基线和现有细粒度方法，且训练时间不到一小时。注意力权重还显示出合理的跨解剖上下文路由。

Conclusion: ACA是一种轻量级有效的方法，能够在保留和增强全局解剖上下文的同时，将CT基础模型适应于解剖级的视觉-语言对齐。

Abstract: CT vision-language foundation models have demonstrated promising performance across downstream tasks, but are typically trained with whole-volume representations that dilute fine-grained anatomical signals. Fine-grained vision-language pre-training addresses this by aligning anatomy-level visual features with anatomy-specific text, but in doing so discards the global context that whole-volume models provide. Furthermore, existing fine-grained approaches train from scratch, making them computationally expensive. We introduce Anatomy Contextualized Adaptation (ACA), a lightweight framework that adapts frozen CT foundation model representations for anatomy-level vision-language alignment while enhancing global contextualization. ACA uses TotalSegmentator to decompose CT volumes into anatomy-level embeddings, which are refined via a transformer that captures cross-anatomy relationships, and aligned to both per-anatomy and scan-level text extracted from radiology reports. Evaluated on Merlin and CT-RATE, ACA consistently outperforms both the frozen foundation model baselines and existing fine-grained methods in zero-shot finding classification, while requiring less than one hour of training once embeddings are cached. The attention weights learned by ACA's inter-anatomy transformer additionally indicate plausible cross-anatomy context routing. Altogether, these results support ACA as a lightweight approach for adapting CT foundation models to anatomically grounded vision-language alignment while preserving and enhancing global anatomical context.

</details>


### [74] [CinemaTraj: Composing Atomic Camera Trajectories for 3D Scenes with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26910)
*Qianru Li,Xuyang Chen,Erkin Türköz,Lu Liu,Xuqin Wang,Liqiu Meng,Tao Wu,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CinemaTraj是一个利用大语言模型和3D场景图，从自然语言描述自动生成电影级摄像机轨迹的框架，在真实环境中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么缺乏真正的3D空间感知（依赖2D图像先验），要么将轨迹生成视为与电影语义无关的几何路径规划问题。本文旨在弥合自然语言描述与电影级摄像机轨迹生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出CinemaTraj框架，将LLM代理与结构化3D场景图结合，将用户提示分解为原子电影摄影运动序列，每个运动通过参数化轨迹表示实例化，支持碰撞规避优化，并同步生成配音和字幕。

Result: 在真实ScanNet++环境的评估中，CinemaTraj在提示对齐度、轨迹质量和安全性指标上均优于现有方法，生成了符合提示、无碰撞且具有高电影质量的轨迹。

Conclusion: 将摄像机轨迹规划重新定义为语言驱动的空间推理问题可以更有效地结合电影语义和3D几何信息，为自动化电影级虚拟内容创作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatically generating cinematically expressive camera trajectories through 3D scenes from natural language descriptions is a challenging task of high practical value, with applications ranging from real-estate advertising to virtual tour creation. Existing methods either lack true 3D spatial awareness by relying on 2D image priors, or treat trajectory generation as a geometric path planning problem divorced from cinematographic semantics. We present CinemaTraj, a framework that reframes camera trajectory planning as a language-grounded spatial reasoning problem. Given a set of RGB-D images and a user prompt, CinemaTraj equips an LLM agent with a structured 3D scene graph: the agent decomposes the prompt into a sequence of atomic cinematographic movements (dolly, orbit, crane, pan, tilt, zoom, arc). Each movement is instantiated via a novel parametric trajectory representation that is both cinematographically expressive and optimizable for collision avoidance. The scene graph acts as a structured spatial prior, grounding the agent's reasoning in accurate geometric and semantic knowledge of the environment. CinemaTraj further generates synchronized voiceover and subtitles aligned with camera motion, producing narrated cinematic video outputs. We evaluate CinemaTraj on real-world ScanNet++ environments, and show that it produces prompt-faithful, collision-free trajectories with high cinematographic quality, outperforming existing approaches on prompt alignment, trajectory quality, and safety metrics.

</details>


### [75] [Prior Directions: Why GUI Grounding Gets Locked in the Past](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26913)
*Weile Gong,Zijian Lu,Mingcai Chen,Yiping Zuo,Xin He,Weibei Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了视觉-语言模型中的"视觉锁定"现象：当场景变化后，模型中过时的语言描述会干扰正确的视觉判断。研究发现，锁定效应与先验信息在模型表示空间中沿特定方向（Prior Directions）的集中程度相关，移除这些方向的分量可以恢复视觉基础判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型常依赖先前的视觉状态描述来决策当前场景，当场景变化时，过时的语言描述会误导模型做出错误判断。论文旨在理解这种"视觉锁定"失效的机制及其产生原因。

Method: 在可控的定位任务（grounding）中仅改变语言先验信息，测量模型锁定强度与表示空间变化的关系。通过识别"Prior Directions"（跨样本重复出现的先验诱导变化方向），进行对比实验和干预实验（移除对齐/正交分量）。

Result: 锁定越强的模型，先验诱导的表示变化越集中于少量重复出现的方向（Prior Directions）。移除这些方向的成分可恢复视觉基础判断，而移除等量的正交成分几乎无效。该模式可在未见样本上泛化。

Conclusion: 先验控制（锁定）的产生源于先验诱导的变化在回答所用的表示空间中形成了连贯且可复用的模式。这解释了为什么相同的先验信息在某个模型中可被修正，在另一个模型中却占据主导。

Abstract: Vision-language models often use descriptions of earlier visual states to make decisions about the current scene. When the scene changes, stale language can redirect an otherwise correct visual judgment toward an outdated answer. We study this failure as visual lock-in in a controlled grounding setting where only the verbalized prior varies. Across models, stronger lock-in accompanies smaller changes in the model representation before the final answer. This reversal suggests that lock-in depends not on how far this representation moves, but on how that movement is organized. In models that are harder to correct, prior-induced changes concentrate along a compact set of directions that repeatedly appear across examples. We call these recurrent axes the Prior Directions. They recur on held-out examples, while a descriptive four-model comparison associates greater concentration with stronger lock-in. Controlled interventions show that removing the component aligned with the Prior Directions restores visual grounding, whereas removing an equally large orthogonal component has little effect. Prior control thus arises when prior-induced changes form a coherent and reusable pattern in the representation used to produce the answer. This account explains why the same prior remains revisable in one model yet becomes dominant in another.

</details>


### [76] [From Keypoints to Predictive Distributions: Post-Hoc Uncertainty for YOLO-Pose Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26921)
*Alexej Klushyn,Juan Rivero Sesma,Florian Seligmann,Richard Kurle,Kinh Tieu,Jayant Sen Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级后验概率扩展方法，为YOLO-Pose模型添加校准的二维双变量预测分布，量化关键点空间不确定性，并在COCO和航空着陆任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YOLO-Pose能够高效定位关键点，但缺乏对关键点空间不确定性的量化，限制了其在安全关键场景中的应用。

Method: 在训练好的YOLO-Pose模型上添加概率预测头，通过重要性加权负对数似然训练预测关键点的2×2色散矩阵，并分别使用高斯校准（通用兼容）和学生t校准（分布拟合）进行后处理。同时提出评估协议：分布校准诊断+平均关键点精度（AKP）。

Result: COCO实验表明：学习到的不确定性估计能有效排序关键点可靠性；学生t校准最好地拟合了残差分布；基于不确定性的剪枝可去除不可靠关键点。航空着陆任务中，校准后的协方差矩阵支持不确定性感知的飞机位置估计与传感器融合。

Conclusion: 后验不确定性量化可低成本地增强YOLO-Pose模型，提升关键点可靠性评估能力，对安全关键应用具有实际价值。

Abstract: YOLO-Pose models provide efficient keypoint localization, but do not quantify the associated spatial uncertainty. We introduce a lightweight post-hoc probabilistic extension that augments a trained YOLO-Pose model with calibrated bivariate predictive distributions over keypoint locations, centered at the model's original predictions. Concretely, we train additional probabilistic heads with an importance-weighted negative log-likelihood to predict an input-dependent $2\times2$ dispersion matrix for each keypoint, followed by Gaussian calibration for broad downstream compatibility or Student-$t$ calibration for distributional fidelity. Complementing this, we propose an evaluation protocol that combines a suite of distributional calibration diagnostics with average keypoint precision (AKP), a keypoint-level extension of the COCO AP protocol for assessing reliability rankings. Experiments on COCO show that the learned uncertainty estimates enable effective keypoint-level reliability ranking, Student-$t$ calibration best captures the empirical residual distribution, and uncertainty-based pruning removes unreliable keypoints. A central application-level demonstration is vision-based aircraft landing, where calibrated covariances for runway keypoints support uncertainty-aware aircraft position estimation and downstream sensor fusion.

</details>


### [77] [Robust RPC Bundle Adjustment for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery with Season-Invariant Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26973)
*Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Xavier Bou,Thibaud Ehret,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度学习局部特征匹配和全局图像描述符的RPC模型精化流程，能够在无地面控制点的多时相卫星影像中，应对季节、光照和地表覆盖变化带来的匹配困难，显著提升几何精度并减少匹配时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RPC光束法平差依赖手工特征匹配，在多时相影像（存在季节、光照、地表覆盖变化）中匹配不可靠，导致几何精化效果差。

Method: 结合学习型局部特征匹配（用于季节不变的对应点提取）与全局图像描述符（用于筛选视觉兼容的图像对），减少冗余和错误匹配，保持匹配图连通性。

Result: 在季节多样的WorldView-3影像上，相比开源基线方法，几何一致性误差更低，且在39-42景影像集上显著缩短匹配时间。

Conclusion: 该方法使RPC精化对时间变化的外观差异更鲁棒，有助于更有效地利用多时相卫星影像。

Abstract: Accurate refinement of Rational Polynomial Camera (RPC) models is essential for high-quality satellite image geolocation. In ground control point (GCP)-free multi-view pipelines, this refinement is commonly performed through bundle adjustment from automatically extracted image correspondences. However, conventional RPC bundle adjustment pipelines rely on handcrafted feature matching, which becomes unreliable in multi-date collections affected by seasonal, illumination, and land-cover changes. We propose an appearance-aware RPC refinement pipeline that combines learned local feature matching for season-invariant correspondences with global image descriptors for selecting visually compatible image pairs. This reduces redundant and error-prone matching while preserving the connectivity of the matching graph. Experiments on seasonally diverse WorldView-3 images show that our pipeline improves GCP-free relative RPC refinement over open-source baselines, achieving lower geometric consistency errors while substantially reducing matching time on collections with 39-42 views. By making RPC refinement more robust to diachronic appearance variation, our approach enables more effective use of multi-date satellite imagery.

</details>


### [78] [Mitigating Compounding Error via Video Representation Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27036)
*Taiye Chen,Qi Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视频扩散世界模型在自回归推理中，误差累积与隐藏表示的维度坍塌（有效秩下降）密切相关，并提出视频表示正则化方法，显著提升了长视频生成的稳定性与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动窗口自回归推理在视频扩散世界模型中存在严重的误差累积，导致帧质量随时间下降。尽管这一现象被广泛观察到，但其底层机制（复合误差如何产生以及如何实现稳定长程生成）尚未得到充分解释。

Method: 1. 研究视频世界模型的内部表示动态，发现复合误差与隐藏表示的维度坍塌（有效秩sharp下降）紧密耦合。2. 验证纯数据规模扩展无法提升模型抵御误差漂移的能力。3. 提出视频表示正则化（video representation regularization），一种轻量级训练约束，用于稳定潜在表示并抑制迭代误差累积。

Result: 与Diffusion Forcing相比，在VBench的Aesthetic Quality指标上从38.65提升至55.56，Imaging Quality指标从44.37提升至72.08，效果显著。

Conclusion: 首次建立了自回归视频漂移与模型内部表示之间的关联，采用有效秩（erank）作为误差累积的定量指标，揭示了视频世界模型中反直觉的扩展限制，并提出了一种简单有效的正则化策略来提升长视频生成的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video diffusion-based world models enable long autoregressive video generation for robotics, autonomous driving and simulation tasks, yet sliding-window autoregressive inference suffers from severe error accumulation that degrades frame quality over time. Although this phenomenon has been widely observed, the underlying mechanism of compounding error and how to achieve stable long-horizon generation remain largely unresolved. In this paper, we investigate the internal representation dynamics of video world models and discover that compounding error is tightly coupled with dimensional collapse of hidden representations. Specifically, the effective rank of model representations sharply decreases at the onset of generation drift, revealing a strong connection between representational degradation and long-term rollout instability. Furthermore, we find that pure training data scaling fails to boost model resistance to error drift, contradicting mainstream scaling paradigms. To address this problem, we propose video representation regularization, a lightweight training constraint that stabilizes latent representations and suppresses iterative error accumulation. Compared with Diffusion Forcing, our method achieves improvements from 38.65 to 55.56 and from 44.37 to 72.08 on the Aesthetic Quality and Imaging Quality metrics of VBench. Our work establishes the first connection between autoregressive video drifting and model internal representations, adopts erank as a quantitative metric for error accumulation, reveals counterintuitive scaling limitations for video world models, and presents a simple yet effective regularization strategy to improve long video generation robustness.

</details>


### [79] [Object Detection for Autonomous Driving in Chinese Rural Scenes: An Experimental Study on Real-Synthetic Data Mixing and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27058)
*Danning Zhu,Ziyan Lin,Jing Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对中国乡村复杂交通场景中自动驾驶目标检测面临的数据稀缺和泛化挑战，提出了一个基于河南尉氏县真实图像与虚幻引擎虚拟场景混合的乡村道路目标检测数据集，定义了14类（含电动三轮车、低速车、路边摊等地域特色类别）目标体系，并在13种主流检测器（YOLOv5/YOLOv8/YOLO11/YOLO26系列及RT-DETR-L）上进行了系统评估。实验表明，适度注入合成数据（真实:虚拟=1:0.5）可有效提升检测性能，其中YOLO11m取得最高mAP@0.5（0.758），但更高比例的合成数据（1:1）会因域偏移抵消数据规模带来的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶目标检测模型在中国乡村复杂交通场景中面临数据严重匮乏和泛化能力不足的问题，亟需针对性的数据集构建与模型评估研究。

Method: 1）构建了真实图像（河南尉氏县采集）与虚幻引擎虚拟场景混合的乡村道路检测数据集；2）定义了包含电动三轮车、低速车、路边摊等14类乡村特有目标的类别体系；3）在统一训练协议下，系统评估了YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11、YOLO26系列及RT-DETR-L共13种检测器在三种数据配置（全真实、1:0.5真实-虚拟混合、1:1混合）下的性能。

Result: 1）适度注入合成数据（1:0.5比例）能有效提升检测性能，YOLO11m取得最佳mAP@0.5（0.758）；2）过高比例合成数据（1:1）因域偏移导致性能增益被抵消；3）大多数模型能可靠识别典型乡村车辆，但对摊位、栏杆等长尾非标准目标仍存在显著感知瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本研究为乡村自动驾驶场景的模型选择与合成数据策略提供了关键实证依据和新见解，有助于推动自动驾驶感知系统在乡村地区的实际部署。

Abstract: Currently, autonomous driving object detection models face significant data scarcity and generalization challenges when navigating complex Chinese rural traffic scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a novel real-synthetic mixed object detection dataset tailored specifically for Chinese rural roads and systematically evaluate the performance of 13 mainstream detectors under different real-to-synthetic data ratios, thereby providing empirical evidence for model selection and data strategy design in rural autonomous driving scenarios. Our dataset combines real-world images captured in Weishi County, Henan Province, with parameterized virtual scenes generated via Unreal Engine. To accurately reflect the unique realities of rural traffic, we define a comprehensive 14-category object system encompassing region-specific elements such as electric tricycles, low-speed vehicles (LSVs), and roadside stalls. Under a unified training protocol, we systematically evaluate 13 mainstream detectors -- spanning the YOLOv5, YOLOv8, YOLO11, and YOLO26 series, as well as RT-DETR-L -- across three data configurations: an all-real baseline, a 1:0.5 real-to-virtual mix, and a 1:1 mix. Experimental results demonstrate that a moderate injection of synthetic data (1:0.5 ratio) effectively enhances detection performance, with YOLO11m achieving the highest mAP@0.5 of 0.758. However, a higher proportion of synthetic data (1:1) introduces domain shifts that offset the benefits of data scaling. While most models reliably identify distinct local vehicles, significant perceptual bottlenecks remain for long-tail, non-standard objects like stalls and railings. This research provides crucial empirical evidence and novel insights for model selection and synthetic data strategies, facilitating the practical deployment of autonomous driving perception systems in rural areas.

</details>


### [80] [Step-Attention Refinement of DINOv3 Features for Efficient Anterior Eye Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27087)
*Philippe Baumstimler,Jean-Mathieu Gagnon,Sébastien Gagné,Mathieu Duchesneau,Clément Playout,Lama Séoud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络的轻量级前眼节分割架构，通过步注意力特征精炼模块逐步适配多层级Transformer表示，在临床数据集上达到85.55% mIoU，并在域迁移场景中展现最强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前眼节（AES）分割是眼生物特征识别和临床图像分析的关键任务，但现有方法在临床环境中面临采集条件异质和标注稀缺两大挑战，导致泛化能力不足。

Method: 基于蒸馏版DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络，提出步注意力特征精炼模块（step-attention feature refinement module），逐步适配多层级Transformer表示，再通过卷积解码器实现高效分割。

Result: 在包含333张临床图像（8种采集协议、7类解剖结构）的私测数据集上，全微调达到85.55% mIoU，优于卷积和Transformer基线；在4个公开数据集上域迁移鲁棒性最强。

Conclusion: 该工作为临床环境下的鲁棒AES分割建立了强基线，并突显了解码器设计对有效适配基础模型表示用于医学分割任务的重要性。

Abstract: Anterior eye segment (AES) segmentation is a key component of both ocular biometrics and emerging clinical image analysis applications. However, heterogeneous acquisition conditions and limited annotations in medical settings hinder the robustness and generalization of existing methods. Foundation models (FMs) such as DINOv3 offer strong transfer capabilities, but efficiently adapting their representations to dense prediction tasks remains challenging. In this study, we investigate robust AES segmentation in clinical settings, and propose a lightweight architecture built upon a distilled DINOv3 ViT-Small backbone. We introduce a step-attention feature refinement module that progressively adapts multi-level transformer representations before convolutional decoding, enabling efficient exploitation of pretrained features with few parameters. We evaluate the proposed approach on a private dataset of 333 clinically acquired AES images spanning eight ophthalmic acquisition protocols and annotated for seven anatomical classes. Compared with convolutional and transformer-based baselines, including DINOv3-based methods, our approach achieves the best overall performance, reaching 85.55\% mIoU when fully fine-tuned. It also demonstrates the strongest robustness to domain shift across four unseen public AES segmentation datasets. These results establish a strong baseline for robust AES segmentation in clinical settings and highlight the importance of decoder design for effectively adapting FMs representations to medical segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [81] [FreqForcing: Autoregressive Long Video Generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27110)
*Jiatong Li,Leo Liang,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqForcing，一种无需训练的视频生成框架，通过频域分析发现自回归视频扩散模型中的误差累积表现为低频能量漂移，并利用频谱自锚定(SSA)技术，将锚点注意力的低频分量用于维持视觉稳定、局部注意力的高频分量用于保持动态运动，从而实现从5秒到2分钟(24倍外推)的长时间视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长时间生成时存在误差累积问题，表现为色彩漂移、运动停滞和视觉坍塌。作者从频域角度表征这一现象，发现误差累积表现为低频带的能量漂移，现有方法(如attention sink)无法完全解决，因此需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出FreqForcing框架，核心是频谱自锚定(Spectral Self-Anchoring, SSA)技术。SSA利用锚点注意力的低频分量维持长期视觉稳定性，同时通过局部注意力的高频分量保留动态运动信息，这是一种无需训练的方法。

Result: 将基于Self-Forcing在5秒片段上预训练的模型扩展到2分钟视频生成，实现24倍外推。大量实验表明FreqForcing在定量和定性上均优于现有无需训练的方法，并与代表性的训练方法竞争。

Conclusion: 频域分析为理解视频生成中的误差累积提供了新视角，FreqForcing通过频谱自锚定有效解决了长时间视频生成中的视觉坍塌问题，无需额外训练即可实现大幅度的时序外推。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable real-time streaming video generation. However, errors introduced during self-rollout accumulate over long horizons, manifesting as color drift, motion stagnation, and eventual visual collapse. In this paper, we characterize this phenomenon from a frequency-domain perspective: error accumulation appears as a pronounced energy drift in the low-frequency bands. We further investigate the effectiveness of attention sink in the frequency domain, and find that it improves the video quality by alleviating the spectral energy drift to some extent, but cannot fully resolve it. Motivated by the above analysis, we propose FreqForcing, a training-free framework that addresses error accumulation in long-video generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring (SSA). The proposed SSA leverages the low-frequency components of anchor attention to maintain long-horizon visual stability, while preserving dynamic motion through the high-frequency components of local attention. Our FreqForcing extends Self-Forcing pretrained on 5s clips to two-minute generation, achieving 24x extrapolation. Extensive experiments show that FreqForcing outperforms existing training-free methods quantitatively and qualitatively while remaining competitive with representative training-based approaches.

</details>


### [82] [Veritas++: Value-aware On-Policy Distillation for Perception-Enhanced AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27113)
*Hao Tan,Jun Lan,Zichang Tan,Ajian Liu,Zijian Yu,Chuanbiao Song,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Wan,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Veritas++框架，通过感知导向学习（PoRL）和价值感知在线蒸馏（VaOPD）增强多模态大模型对AI生成图像的检测能力，在多个基准上取得优异泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的AIGI检测方法存在感知瓶颈，主要关注视觉证据的组织与合成，而内在感知能力未被充分优化，导致难以捕获细粒度异常。

Method: 提出Veritas++框架：1) 感知导向学习（PoRL）：用可验证奖励替代开放式描述监督，增强模型对细粒度视觉细节、语义异常和像素级差异的感知；2) 价值感知在线蒸馏（VaOPD）：通过特权自教师机制，自适应蒸馏高价值信号，将增强的感知与推理内化整合。

Result: 在标准、野外和新出现基准上，Veritas++实现优秀的泛化性能，感知学习有效弥合感知差距并带来检测增益，VaOPD在不牺牲已有性能的前提下实现高效能力进化。

Conclusion: 感知增强推理框架能够有效解决MLLM在AIGI检测中的感知瓶颈，通过PoRL和VaOPD的组合策略可显著提升检测的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The growing capability of image generation models has made synthetic images a routine presence in open media, making robust and generalizable AI-Generated Image (AIGI) detection increasingly essential. While multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) offer a transparent alternative to black-box binary scoring, we observe that current MLLM-based detectors still exhibit notable perception bottlenecks in capturing fine-grained anomalies. They primarily focus on how visual evidence is organized and synthesized, leaving the intrinsic perception less optimized. To mitigate this gap, we present Veritas++, a perception-enhanced reasoning framework that establishes reliable perception as the foundation of authenticity reasoning. Rather than directly optimizing the model's explanatory ability, we ground AIGI detection on three basic perception abilities, i.e., capturing fine-grained visual details, semantic anomalies and pixel-level differences. Building on this insight, we introduce Perception-oriented Learning (PoRL), which replaces open-ended description supervision with verifiable rewards to explicitly strengthen these capacities. To further integrate enhanced perception with reasoning, we introduce Value-aware On-Policy Distillation (VaOPD), an adaptive distillation mechanism that prioritizes high-value distillation signals over uniform supervision, internalizing perception-aware reasoning through a privileged self-teacher. Extensive experiments across standard, in-the-wild and emerging benchmarks demonstrate that Veritas++ achieves promising generalization. The perception learning effectively bridges the perception gap and yields seamless gains on detection, while VaOPD further enables efficient capability evolvement without sacrificing existing performance. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/EricTan7/VeritasPP.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Grounded GI Endoscopy VQA via Multi-Task Learning on Small VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27122)
*Itbaan Safwan,Ramail Khan,Muhammad Annas Shaikh,Muhammad Atif Tahir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务微调方法，通过重用现有VQA数据集中的标注（息肉掩膜）和分类器弱监督定位，提升VLM在胃肠道内镜图像问答中的准确率与隐式视觉对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床场景不仅要求VLM对胃肠道内镜图像中的自由形式临床问题给出正确答案，还要求模型内部表征能够反映答案背后的视觉证据，但现有方法缺乏对这种隐式对齐的关注。

Method: 提出多任务微调方案：1) 直接复用专家标注的息肉掩膜作为定位任务；2) 利用GI领域预训练分类器+Grad-CAM生成弱监督定位信号，用于缺乏真实掩膜的类别。在Kvasir-VQA-x1数据集上，对三个小型VLM骨干采用低秩适配（LoRA）进行VLQA-only和多任务对比微调。

Result: 多任务微调在分布内和分布外数据上均一致提升了答案准确率，同时改善了答案token与相关图像区域之间的隐式对齐。

Conclusion: 简单的多任务微调策略能有效增强VLM在医学VQA任务上的性能与可解释性，且无需大量额外标注成本。

Abstract: Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopic image analysis has shifted from single-label classification toward visual question answering (VQA), where a model must answer free-form clinical questions about an image. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) achieve promising answer accuracy on this task, clinical adoption also requires the model's internal representations to reflect the visual evidence behind its answers. We propose a simple multi-task fine-tuning recipe that constructs auxiliary grounding and description tasks from an existing VQA dataset with minimal additional annotation: expert-annotated polyp masks are reused directly, while a GI-domain pretrained classifier with Grad-CAM localization provides weak supervision for finding categories that lack ground-truth masks. Three small VLM backbones are fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation under matched VQA-only and multi-task recipes on Kvasir-VQA-x1, and we show consistent accuracy gains together with improved implicit alignment between answer tokens and the relevant image region, evaluated on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [84] [SeasonStereo: Robust Dense Stereo Matching for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery via Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27139)
*Álvaro Díaz-Laureano,Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Pablo Arias,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeasonStereo是一种可扩展的框架，通过合成图像对训练和基础模型的零样本几何先验，实现了对多日期卫星图像的视差估计，无需LiDAR标签即可达到与最先进方法相当的精度，为大规模异构卫星图像3D重建提供了低监督成本的实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多日期卫星图像因季节和光照变化导致外观差异大，难以直接进行立体匹配的问题；同时降低对对齐的真实多日期训练数据和LiDAR标签的高昂获取成本。

Method: 在合成图像对上训练模型，控制季节外观变化；利用基础模型的零样本几何先验增强泛化能力。

Result: 匹配了最先进的LiDAR监督模型的精度，同时生成了更锐利的几何细节，且无需真实多日期训练产品或LiDAR标签。

Conclusion: SeasonStereo为大规模异构卫星图像3D重建提供了一条低监督成本的实用路径。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction from satellite imagery typically relies on near-simultaneous stereo pairs, limiting its applicability to diachronic settings where multi-date images exhibit varying seasonal and illumination conditions. Training dense stereo matching models robust to appearance changes is a long-standing challenge, as aligned multi-date imagery and ground-truth geometry are costly to obtain at scale. We propose SeasonStereo, a scalable framework that addresses disparity estimation from diachronic satellite images by training on synthetic image pairs with controlled seasonal appearance variation, while leveraging zero-shot geometric priors from foundation models. SeasonStereo matches the accuracy of state-of-the-art LiDAR-supervised models, while producing sharper geometric details without requiring aligned real multi-date training products or LiDAR-derived labels. As a result, SeasonStereo offers a practical path toward large-scale 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous satellite images with reduced supervision cost.

</details>


### [85] [Explainable and Resource-Efficient Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs for Decision-Critical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27145)
*Piyush Jain,Kousik Dasgupta,Rajarshi Roy,Subarna Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ByDeWay-V2 是一种无需训练的框架，通过在 MLLM 提示中注入显式的空间关系谓词（如"左边"、"内部"等）来提升多模态大模型的空间理解能力，在 BLINK 空间子集上相比先前方法 LDP 实现了 46% 的 F1 相对提升，并能在 CPU 端以极低 token 预算运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在机器人、具身AI等决策关键场景中缺乏空间判断的可解释性与可审计性，且现有方法（如 LDP）仅用粗粒度的深度分层，无法有效区分同一几何平面内物体之间的投射关系和拓扑关系。

Method: 使用开放词汇目标检测器（YOLO-World-L）计算检测物体间的成对几何关系，将这些关系转化为人类可读的结构化空间谓词，与深度线索一并注入到 MLLM 提示中，整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在 BLINK 空间子集上，ByDeWay-V2 相比 LDP 在 Qwen2.5-VL 上 F1 相对提升 46%；在 VSR 上将 BLIP-Base 从接近随机性能恢复到 0.53 的 F1；最轻配置在 CPU 上仅用 40 token 上下文即可运行。

Conclusion: 显式注入空间关系谓词能够有效弥补 MLLM 在细粒度空间理解上的不足，且该方法轻量、无需训练，适用于资源受限的实时决策支持场景。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in decision-critical pipelines such as robotics, embodied AI, and safety monitoring, the opacity of their spatial judgments limits operator trust and auditability. MLLMs demonstrate strong reasoning but often struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and object hallucination. Prior work, ByDeWay, introduced Layered-Depth-Based Prompting (LDP), a training-free framework that mitigates hallucinations by structuring prompts using monocular depth estimation. However, coarse depth layering falls short in resolving object-to-object spatial relationships within the same geometric plane, such as projective ("left of", "above") and topological ("inside", "touching") relations. We propose ByDeWay-V2, which integrates explicit spatial relational context alongside depth cues, expressed as human-readable predicates that serve as auditable evidence for downstream decision support. Using an open-vocabulary object detector (YOLO-World-L), our framework computes pairwise geometric relations between detected objects and injects them as structured spatial predicates into the MLLM prompt, bridging 3D scene depth and 2D spatial semantics without any training. We evaluate ByDeWay-V2 on the Visual Spatial Reasoning (VSR) and BLINK benchmarks across multiple MLLMs, with hallucination grounding assessed via POPE. On the BLINK spatial subset, ByDeWay-V2 achieves a 46 percent relative F1 improvement over LDP for Qwen2.5-VL, and recovers BLIP-Base's spatial reasoning on VSR from near-random performance to a competitive F1 of 0.53. Our lightest configuration operates under a strict 40-token context budget on CPU, showing the framework's suitability for resource-constrained, real-time decision-support settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Towards Trustworthy Embodied Intelligence: A Systems Framework and Graded Trustworthiness Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26121)
*Xinyu Yang,Tianxing Chen,Honghao Su,Minxuan Wang,Chenze Yu,Zhangzheng Tu,Yue Chen,Yuxiao Huo,Lingfeng Zhang,Yan Huang,Yan Qin,Shaolong Zhu,Qiwei Liang,Hekun Tian,Shujia Liu,Guangyu Chen,Junhao Gong,Zixuan Li,Wenwei Lin,Zijian Lin,Wenxuan Zhu,Eric J Chen,Yue Yuan,Qize Yu,Jiaqi Liang,Haowen Yan,Hengfei Zhao,Weijie Wan,Zikun Xiao,Junyuan Tang,Baijun Chen,Kai-Chong Lei,Kaixuan Wang,Kailun Su,Zanxin Chen,Yao Mu,Renjing Xu,Chuqiao Lyu,Qi Xiong,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出可信具身智能的概念——持续安全成功（sustained safe success），即系统在环境和系统变化下可靠执行任务且将风险控制在可接受范围内。为此构建了模型层、系统层、证据层、部署层四层机制，并提出非规范化的可信等级层次，用于分级部署、比较评估、研究优先级和未来标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中任务完成不等于可信，因为失败可能造成直接物理或操作危害。现有方法缺乏系统性的可信定义和分层保障机制。

Method: 定义"持续安全成功"目标，构建四层支撑机制（模型层、系统层、证据层、部署层），并提出非规范化可信等级层次。

Result: 提出了一个涵盖四层机制和可信等级层次的理论框架，用于评估和保障具身智能系统的端到端可信性。

Conclusion: 模型能力、孤立安全措施或基准性能均不足以建立端到端可信性，需要跨层次协同保障。提出的等级层次为分级部署、比较评估和未来标准化提供了基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence integrates learned perception and decision making with real-time computation, control, and physical interaction. Because failures can cause immediate physical or operational harm, task completion alone does not establish trustworthiness. We define trustworthy embodied intelligence as the sustained capacity to execute specified tasks reliably under environmental and system variation while maintaining risk within acceptable bounds. We term this objective sustained safe success. Its supporting mechanisms are organized into four interdependent layers. The model layer generates task-competent action proposals with calibrated uncertainty and explicit safety preferences. The system layer realizes authorized actions dependably through integrated sensing, computation, control, hardware safeguards, fault containment, and fallback. The evidence layer substantiates bounded claims through evaluation, verification, validation, traceability, and structured assurance arguments. The deployment layer maintains claim validity through runtime monitoring, authority management, intervention, incident response, and controlled updates. Because assumptions and failures propagate across these layers, neither model capability, isolated safeguards, nor benchmark performance alone can establish end-to-end trustworthiness. Drawing on embodied AI, robotics, control, dependable computing, distributed systems, and autonomous driving, we further propose a non-normative hierarchy of trustworthiness levels. This hierarchy grades the strength of bounded deployment claims across task capability, safety, system assurance, operational governance, and supporting evidence, providing a basis for bounded deployment, comparative evaluation, research prioritization, and future standardization.

</details>


### [87] [Embodied Agents Take Control: Minimal-Interface Zero-Shot Agents Rival Industrial-Scale Policies in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26148)
*Jian Zhou,Xunyi Zhao,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Sihao Lin,Jiajun Liu,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究自主具身智能体的"智能体控制"范式，即通用型智能体自行维持感知-行动-验证-自我纠正的决策循环。在零样本导航测试中，默认配置的复制可达到70.7±3.5%成功率（opus-5），而混合系统（fable-5+waypoint工具）在默认配置下达到76.7±0.6%成功率，且环境步数和耗时显著减少。实验表明能力主要取决于模型选择，而强制接口对弱模型有帮助但可能阻碍强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主具身智能体依赖任务特定工作流或具身策略维持决策循环，缺乏通用性。本文探索第三种范式——智能体控制，即让通用智能体自行维持整个循环，以零样本导航为测试床评估其可行性。

Method: 在零样本导航场景下，使用仅有单目RGB相机和离散动作的极简条件，评估三种软件工程智能体框架（harnesses）。引入可选的训练好的waypoint工具，比较默认配置与最大配置下的性能、步数和耗时。

Result: 默认配置下opus-5达70.7±3.5%成功率，fable-5最大配置达78%。混合系统fable-5+waypoint工具默认配置达76.7±0.6%，步数减半、耗时减少75%。能力主要取决于模型选择，框架效果是描述性的，强制waypoint接口帮助弱模型但阻碍强模型。长程任务性能显著下降，延迟和上下文增长限制持续运行。

Conclusion: 智能体控制范式在零样本导航中已具备竞争力，模型、框架和接口是通向自主具身智能体的互补路径。

Abstract: Autonomous embodied agents must sustain a long decision-making loop that involves perceiving, acting, verifying, and self-correcting over many steps. Current systems sustain this loop through task-specific workflows or embodied policies. We study a third form, agentic embodied control, in which a general-purpose agent holds the loop itself. Using zero-shot navigation as a controlled testbed, we evaluate three software-engineering agent harnesses given only a monocular RGB camera and discrete actions. Under this strictly minimal condition, replicated default-effort configurations reach 70.7$\pm$3.5% success (opus-5, mean over three runs), and fable-5 reaches 78% at maximum effort. When a trained waypoint tool is exposed alongside primitives as an optional capability, the hybrid fable-5 agent reaches 76.7$\pm$0.6% at default effort, using half the environment steps and less than one quarter of the wall time of the maximum-effort primitive run. Controlled interventions show that capability is primarily model-centered: model choice strongly changes success, harness effects are descriptive, and a forced waypoint interface helps weaker models but can hinder stronger ones. Performance nevertheless falls sharply on longer-horizon tasks, while latency and context growth limit sustained operation. These results show that agentic control is already competitive in zero-shot navigation and that models, harnesses, and interfaces offer complementary paths toward autonomous embodied agents.

</details>


### [88] [Multi-Objective Compliance-Integrated Coevolution For Simulated And Real-World Deployment Of Multi-Robot Marine Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26279)
*Everardo Gonzalez,Tyler M. Paine,Manuel Agraz Vallejo,Gaurav Dixit,Michael R. Benjamin,Kagan Tumer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Marine Multi-Objective Compliance-Integrated Coevolution (MMOCIC)框架，通过将协同进化行为与合规行为融合，在最大化团队进度与最小化规范违规之间取得平衡，并在最多8台水下机器人（硬件）和12台（仿真）的协作救援任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同进化算法虽能处理稀疏反馈信号并生成多目标协调行为，但难以在运行中动态融合高层团队目标与低层合规考量（如安全规范），导致规范遵守与团队性能之间的平衡难以实现。

Method: 提出MMOCIC框架，核心思想是将学习过程与合规行为解耦（因为操作规范是预设而非发现的），通过融合协同进化行为与合规行为，实现团队进度最大化与规范违规最小化的多目标优化。

Result: 在协作游泳者救援任务中，硬件部署实现8台水下机器人、仿真实现12台机器人的规模，系统在保持高性能的同时避免了碰撞。

Conclusion: MMOCIC框架成功将团队优化与预设规范融合，为基于学习的协调行为在真实海事任务中的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are well-suited to maritime missions that benefit from coordination, such as the exploration of unknown reef structures, inspection of subsea infrastructure, or search-and-rescue operations. These missions typically provide sparse feedback signals for measuring progress and require adherence to safety and regulatory norms, turning a mission into a multi-objective optimization problem. Coevolutionary algorithms can process these sparse feedback signals to generate coordinated behaviors, and in some cases extend behaviors to multiple objectives. However, incorporating high-level team objectives with low-level compliance considerations on the fly to balance norm adherence with team performance remains elusive. This paper introduces a multi-objective framework that blends coevolved behaviors with compliance behaviors to achieve a balance between maximizing team progress and minimizing norm violations. The key insight is to decouple learning from compliance since operational norms are prescribed rather than discovered. We demonstrate that our framework achieves high team performance while avoiding collisions on a collaborative swimmer rescue mission with up to 8 vehicles in a hardware deployment, and 12 vehicles in simulation. The key contribution of this paper is Marine Multi-Objective Compliance-Integrated Coevolution (MMOCIC), a framework that blends team-wide optimization with established norms for real-world deployments of learning-based coordination.

</details>


### [89] [MoMo: Dial Motion Mode in Robot Manipulation with Spatiotemporal Action Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26315)
*Yuhan Hu,Hugues Thomas,Peide Huang,Mouli Sivapurapu,Benoit Landry,Arto Kivila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MoMo框架，通过两阶段模仿学习将机器人操作中的运动模式（如平稳、动态）作为可复用行为因子，实现跨任务的动作风格迁移与组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在不同场景下不仅完成操作任务，还要适应性地调整动作执行方式（如速度、力度等），现有方法缺乏对这种执行层面变化的学习与复用机制。

Method: 提出MoMo两阶段框架：1）时空动作分词器提取运动模式；2）以任务和连续运动模式条件为输入的行为克隆Transformer，在6种真实机器人操作任务上训练。

Result: 不同运动模式条件产生稳定、动态及中间态行为，人类评估者可区分；在仅有一种模式演示的任务上，MoMo能迁移未见模式并保持任务成功率。

Conclusion: 运动模式可作为跨任务复用的行为因子，实现未见任务-模式组合的组合泛化，控制操作技能的执行方式。

Abstract: To operate effectively across diverse contexts, robots must not only perform manipulation tasks accurately but also adapt how their actions unfold to the task, object, and interaction setting. We ask whether this execution-level variation can be learned as a reusable behavioral factor shared across tasks. We present \textbf{MoMo}, a two-stage imitation-learning framework consisting of a spatiotemporal action tokenizer and a behavior-cloning transformer that takes task and a continuous motion-mode condition as inputs. Across six real-robot manipulation tasks, varying this condition produces steady, dynamic, and intermediate behaviors that human raters can distinguish and that differ in joint speed, acceleration, and end-effector approach pitch. On tasks demonstrated in only one mode, MoMo transfers the unseen requested mode while largely preserving task success. Together, these results provide evidence of compositional generalization to unseen task--mode combinations and show that motion mode can be reused across tasks to control how a manipulation skill is performed.

</details>


### [90] [Reeling It In: Flexible Needle Pick Up via Thread Manipulation for Autonomous Suturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26337)
*Emma Huang,Zih-Yun Chiu,Neelay Joglekar,Shanglei Liu,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用缝合线间接拾取缝针的自主框架，解决了传统方法在针被遮挡、不可直接接触或位于组织上时存在的安全性和可行性问题，并在达芬奇研究套件上验证了其在复杂现实条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主缝合过程中，缝针可能意外掉落或需要调整抓取姿态，现有方法要求针可见且可直接接近，且抓取位于组织上的针时易夹伤组织或导致针弹跳，存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出一个利用缝合线作为辅助工具的间接拾针框架，涵盖线及组织重建、安全抓取点选择、稳定提线、双臂跟线至成功抓针的完整流程，并引入视觉不确定性建模以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在达芬奇研究套件上的多种真实场景实验中，该方法即使在复杂线配置或针不可接近的条件下也表现出稳健性能。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了自主机器人策略在非结构化缝合环境中应用的差距，通过间接方式提升了拾针的安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Suture-needle pickup is necessary for autonomous suturing, as a needle can be unexpectedly dropped or strategically released to adjust the grasping configuration. Current methods for autonomous needle pickup typically guide a robot to move straight toward the needle and grasp it, limited to conditions where the needle is observable and directly approachable. In addition, grasping the needle lying on tissue can lead to the robot pinching nearby tissue or the needle jumping around due to its slippery surface, posing potential safety issues. This work proposes an autonomous framework that uses a suture thread as an assistive tool for indirect needle pickup, avoiding unnecessary tool-tissue contact and enabling pickup even when the needle is occluded or inaccessible. The framework spans the entire workflow, including thread and tissue reconstruction, safe grasp-point selection, stable thread lifting, and bimanual thread-following until securing needle grasping. The robot policies account for visual uncertainty to maximize robustness in real-world environments. We evaluate the proposed framework on a da Vinci Research Kit under various real-world conditions. The results demonstrate robust performance even with a challenging thread configuration or a non-approachable needle, closing the gap in applying autonomous robot policies to unstructured suturing environments.

</details>


### [91] [Self-Adaptive Learning and Model Predictive Control for Tracking Unknown Dynamics with No Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26370)
*Atharva Navsalkar,Hongyu Zhou,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应在线学习控制方法，用于追踪未知且可能切换行为的目标动态。该方法通过自监督、单次学习同时学习多个预测器，并自适应选择最优者，具备有限时间近最优性保证，在仿真和硬件实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在动态地图构建、交通控制、追逃等场景中追踪未知动态目标（包括结构化、随机和对抗性混合行为），现有方法难以处理目标动态的切换行为。

Method: 同时从零开始学习多个预测器（自监督、单次学习、计算高效），并自适应选择最佳预测器以匹配观察到的目标行为。

Result: 1）在无学习误差和切换时，渐近匹配知道先验目标动态的最优非因果控制策略（零遗憾）；2）存在误差和切换时性能优雅退化；3）在Crazyflie仿真和硬件实验中优于非随机、核方法和神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅能处理未知且切换的目标动态，还具有理论上的有限时间近最优性保证，实际验证表现优异。

Abstract: We propose a self-adaptive online learning for control method for tracking unknown target dynamics. The target dynamics can exhibit switching behavior, particularly, a mixture of structured, random, and/or adversarial motion. Such challenging target tracking scenarios arise in applications of dynamic mapping, traffic control, and pursuit evasion, where robots need to track, pursue, or avoid collision with moving landmarks, objects, humans, etc., whose dynamics are unknown. Our method simultaneously learns multiple predictors from scratch, via self-supervised, one-shot, and computationally efficient learning, and adaptively selects the best one to match the observed target behavior. The method enjoys finite-time near-optimality guarantees in expectation, characterized as a function of the learning error of the target dynamics and the frequency that the target dynamics switch. In the absence of both error and switching, the method asymptotically matches the optimal non-causal control policy that knows a priori the target dynamics, i.e., the method enjoys no regret in expectation. In the presence of learning errors and switching, the method degrades gracefully, \eg when there are errors and no switching, the average regret is proportional to the average learning error and switching times. To prove these guarantees, a novel technical approach is required compared to the existing works that employ RFF-based online learning. We validate our method in Crazyflie simulations and hardware experiments, across target trajectories that vary from structured to random to adversarial, in comparison to non-stochastic, kernel-based, and neural-network-based methods for online learning.

</details>


### [92] [Reinforcement Learning on Cost-Constrained Quadrupedal Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26434)
*Javier C. Weddington,Bence P. Ölveczky,Stephen A. Baccus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对低成本机器人平台因传输延迟和噪声反馈导致的仿真到现实差距，本文采用生物启发方法，利用平均执行器延迟的前向模型结合时间感知神经网络，在Mini Pupper 2四足机器人上实现了鲁棒的运动控制，并自发学习出类似脊椎动物的中央模式发生器（CPG），可承受+320ms的延迟扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机器人平台存在显著的传输延迟（>50ms）和噪声反馈，导致仿真环境训练的强化学习策略难以直接部署到真实硬件上，需要弥合sim-to-real差距。

Method: 采用生物启发式方法：1）构建平均执行器延迟的前向模型；2）使用时间感知神经网络来应对延迟和噪声反馈；3）在Mini Pupper 2低成本四足平台上进行验证。

Result: 时间感知神经网络实现了鲁棒的 locomotion，并自发学习出中央模式发生器（CPG），可承受高达+320ms的延迟扰动，表现出自我维持的节律步态。

Conclusion: 时间自组织可能是应对成本受限机器人运动控制中延迟问题的通用策略，为在低成本硬件上部署强化学习策略提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Deploying learned control policies on low-cost robotic platforms introduces transport latencies and noisy motor feedback that systematically widens the sim-to-real gap. The chasm of simulation to deployment in hardware lies in the delay of the actuator reaching the commanded position. On platforms such as the Mini Pupper 2, a measured > $50 ms transport delay transforms the locomotion task from a standard Markov decision process into a partially observable one. In this paper, we take a biologically inspired approach of handling noisy and delayed feedback to close the sim-to-real gap, thereby expanding the capability of reinforcement learning on cost-constrained hardware. Using a low-cost quadrupedal hardware platform, we find that using a forward model of the average actuator delay, paired with a time-aware neural network results in robust locomotion. Additionally, our time-aware neural network learned a central pattern generator (CPG): a self-sustaining rhythmic gait that is robust to +320 ms latency perturbations, mirroring the CPGs found in the spinal cords of vertebrates. We posit that temporal self-organization may be a general strategy for cost-constrained locomotion.

</details>


### [93] [RLMM-Flow: A Flow-based Mobile Manipulation Framework with Latent-Space Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26460)
*Shuhang Wang,Ziming Li,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RLMM-Flow框架，结合流策略预训练和潜在空间强化学习后训练，提升移动操作任务中的动作生成质量，优于纯模仿学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成策略可以从专家演示中学习多模态运动先验，但纯模仿训练无法超越演示分布的质量，需要引入强化学习来进一步优化策略。

Method: 先通过专家演示预训练流策略捕获全身运动先验，冻结后引入潜在转向网络优化初始噪声。采用动作空间评论家预热、粗细粒度潜在转向等技术稳定高维优化。

Result: 在移动操作运动规划基准上，RLMM-Flow在任务成功率、碰撞避免和轨迹质量上显著优于纯模仿流策略和现有强化学习后训练基线。

Conclusion: 结合流策略预训练与潜在空间RL后训练能有效提升移动操作策略质量，同时保持快速推理能力。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires generating whole-body action chunks that jointly satisfy goal reaching, collision avoidance, base kinematic constraints, manipulator joint limits, and trajectory smoothness. Flow-based generative policies provide an efficient paradigm for learning multimodal and temporally consistent motion priors from expert demonstrations, but imitation-only training cannot improve policy quality beyond the demonstration distribution. We propose RLMM-Flow, a flow-based mobile manipulation framework that combines expert flow-policy pretraining with latent-space reinforcement learning post-training. The framework first learns a flow policy that captures a multimodal whole-body motion prior from expert demonstrations. The pretrained flow policy is then frozen, while a latent steering network steers its initial noise toward higher-value action chunks. To stabilize high-dimensional latent optimization, we warm up an action-space critic before jointly training the latent critic and latent actor, and introduce coarse-to-fine latent steering that progressively expands control from a horizon-shared latent representation to a full-dimensional residual representation. Experiments on mobile manipulation motion-planning benchmarks show that RLMM-Flow substantially improves task success, collision avoidance, and trajectory quality over imitation-only flow policies and existing reinforcement learning post-training baselines, while preserving fast flow-based inference.

</details>


### [94] [Explicit Kinematic Guidance from Analytic Concepts for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26513)
*Mingyang Sun,Jiude Wei,Xiujian Liang,Qichen He,Donglin Wang,Cewu Lu,Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Concept Expert模块，利用3D信息构建可执行的解析概念（Analytic Concepts），为VLA模型提供结构化、程序化的物体表示，以增强其在复杂操作任务中的空间感知和物理交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖2D输入，忽略了3D物理世界中的物体结构信息和常识知识，限制了其在复杂高精度操作任务中的空间感知和适应能力。

Method: 构建Concept Expert模块，分两个阶段运行：（1）在VLA推理前，利用VFMs估计物体的初始运动学和结构参数；（2）在操作过程中，VLA模型动态跟踪概念参数并与观测对齐。解析概念通过密集程序化操作奖励和精确空间引导为VLA微调提供指导。

Result: 在监督学习和强化学习场景中，该方法在成功率和学习效率上均取得一致的提升，验证了结构化概念引导对VLA后训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于解析概念的引导机制使VLA模型能够学习物理可解释的交互行为，同时保持端到端学习的灵活性，为弥合2D视觉与3D物理世界之间的鸿沟提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely mainly on 2D inputs, neglecting the rich object structural information and commonsense knowledge inherent in the 3D physical world. This deficiency restricts their spatial awareness and adaptability for complex, high-precision manipulation. To bridge this crucial gap, we construct a Concept Expert module for VLA to build executable Analytic Concepts that represent objects as explicit, programmatic blueprints. Our mechanism operates in two synergistic phases: First, prior to VLA inference, the Concept Expert leverages 3D information from Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to estimate the initial kinematic and structural parameters. Second, throughout the manipulation process, the VLA model utilizes its inherent capability to dynamically track the dynamic concept parameters, continuously aligning them with observational changes to ensure persistent accuracy. Once established, the Analytic Concepts provide explicit, high-quality guidance for VLA fine-tuning through (1) dense, programmatic manipulation rewards and (2) precise spatial guidance.
  This formulation allows VLA models to learn physically grounded interaction behaviors while maintaining end-to-end learning flexibility.
  Our experimental results show consistent improvements in success rate and learning efficiency across supervised and reinforcement learning settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured, concept-based guidance for VLA post-training.

</details>


### [95] [Speech2Grasp: Data-Efficient Transfer of Text-Conditioned Grasp Detection to Speech in Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26567)
*Hung Nguyen,Kim Nhat Minh Nguyen,Van Duc Vu,Van-Danh Le,Hoang Huy Le,Dinh Tuan Nguyen,Pham Tuyen Le,Van-Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Speech2Grasp框架，通过轻量级MLP投影器将文本条件的视觉语言模型有效迁移到语音输入，在人形机器人抓取任务中优于级联ASR方案，推理延迟更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型通常假设文本输入，但人形机器人与人类自然交互时语音更为自然。因此，希望将成熟的文本条件模型高效迁移到语音输入，避免从头训练语音模型的高昂成本。

Method: 以ALBEF为案例，诊断分析发现轻量级MLP投影器可有效适配语音输入，同时保持语义区分性和鲁棒性。基于此，提出Speech2Grasp框架，将文本条件的抓取检测迁移到语音。

Result: 实际人形机器人实验表明Speech2Grasp优于级联ASR流水线，且推理延迟更低。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过轻量级适配器将成熟的文本条件系统扩展至语音输入是一种实用范式，具备数据高效的优势。

Abstract: Humanoid robots increasingly require multi-modal understanding for natural interaction with humans. Despite the prominence of vision-language models, they generally assume textual rather than the more natural speech inputs. In this paper, we investigate whether a well-established text-conditioned model can be transferred to speech in a data-efficient manner. Using ALBEF as a case study, we conduct diagnostic analyses showing that a lightweight MLP-based projector effectively adapts it to speech, while preserving semantic discrimination and robustness. Motivated by these findings, we introduce Speech2Grasp, a framework for data-efficient transfer of text-conditioned grasp detection to speech. Real-world humanoid robot experiments show that Speech2Grasp outperforms cascaded ASR-based pipeline, while reducing inference latency. Our findings suggest a practical paradigm for extending established text-conditioned systems to speech.

</details>


### [96] [Semi-Decentralized Multi-Spacecraft Collision Avoidance under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26570)
*Grace Ra Kim,Mahdi Al-Husseini,Duncan Eddy,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将航天器碰撞规避问题建模为半分散部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（SDec-POMDP），通过近似递归小步半分散A*算法求解，在间歇性通信约束下实现接近集中式规划的机动质量，同时减少28.5%的同步事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航天器碰撞规避依赖间歇性地面站联络，导致信息更新延迟且异步，需要在不连续信息共享下规划机动。虽然POMDP能刻画序列不确定性，但现有多智能体扩展要么假设持续信息共享，要么使用不符合地面站约束的通信模型。

Method: 将问题建模为SDec-POMDP，用实际地面站可见窗口控制信息传播；采用近似递归小步半分散A*（RS-SDA*）算法计算联合机动策略。

Result: 在典型交会场景中，半分散规划恢复接近集中式的机动质量，同时比持续协调减少28.5%的同步事件；相比基于规则的算子启发式方法，通信感知规划更一致地达到期望的避碰距离带，同时最小化不必要的轨迹偏离。

Conclusion: 建立了一个在现实间歇通信下实现自主碰撞规避的实用规划框架，弥合了理想化集中协调与完全分散规划执行之间的差距。

Abstract: Current spacecraft collision-avoidance operations rely on intermittent ground-station contacts, requiring operators to plan with delayed and asynchronously updated information. Consequently, maneuvers must be planned with only intermittent information sharing between operators, raising the question of how much coordination is needed to achieve collision-avoidance performance comparable to centralized planning. Although decision-theoretic approaches such as partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) capture the sequential and uncertain nature of collision avoidance, existing multiagent extensions typically assume either continuous information sharing or communication models that do not reflect operational ground-station constraints. To explicitly model this intermittent information availability, we formulate the spacecraft-to-spacecraft collision avoidance problem as a semi-decentralized POMDP (SDec-POMDP), where we govern information propagation directly by realistic ground-station visibility windows. Joint maneuver policies are computed using approximate Recursive Small-Step Semi-Decentralized A* (RS-SDA*), following the state-of-the-art A*-based lineage for decentralized multiagent planning. Across a representative suite of conjunction scenarios, semi-decentralized planning recovers near-centralized maneuver quality while requiring 28.5% fewer synchronization events than continuous coordination. Comparisons with representative rule-based operator heuristics further show that communication-aware planning more consistently achieves the desired operational miss-distance band while minimizing unnecessary trajectory deviation. Together, these results establish a practical planning framework for autonomous collision avoidance under realistic intermittent communication, bridging the gap between idealized centralized coordination and fully decentralized planning execution.

</details>


### [97] [ContactFlow: A video action conditioning that transfers across embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26579)
*Sami Azirar,Enrico Pallotta,Jan Nogga,Jürgen Gall,Sven Behnke,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Contact Flow——一种与具体机械结构无关的动作表征，通过编码3D接触点轨迹实现人类示教到不同机器人形态的迁移，并基于此训练大规模视频生成式世界模型，用于机器人操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型难以捕捉操作中的物理约束（尤其是接触），且动作条件受限于特定机械结构（如平行夹爪），无法迁移到不同机器人形态或人类演示。

Method: 提出Contact Flow动作表征：编码操作者与目标物体间3D接触点的轨迹，丢弃具体外观和运动学信息；基于此训练大规模视频生成模型，并集成到"提议-想象-验证-执行"流水线中，由视觉语言模型评估生成结果。

Result: 在DROID数据集和真实桌面操作任务上，Contact Flow实现了人类演示到不同机器人形态间的迁移，生成物理合理的操作预测。

Conclusion: Contact Flow通过解耦动作表征与具体形态，有效桥接了人类示教与多形态机器人的操作规划，为通用机器人学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: World models offer a promising route toward robot planning by enabling agents to imagine and verify the consequences of actions before execution. However, current video-based world models often struggle to capture the physical constraints that govern manipulation, particularly contact. Further, their action conditioning is often constrained to specific embodiments such as parallel grippers. We propose \emph{Contact Flow}, an embodiment-agnostic action representation that encodes manipulation through the trajectory of 3D contact points between an actor and a target object. By discarding actor-specific appearance and kinematics, Contact Flow provides a shared conditioning signal for both human demonstrations and robotic execution. Therefore, we can train a large-scale video generative model on both human and robotic object interaction videos conditioned on Contact Flow, yielding a world model that predicts physically plausible manipulation outcomes. We integrate this model into a propose-imagine-verify-act pipeline, where generated rollouts are assessed by a vision-language model before execution. Experiments on the DROID dataset and real-world tabletop manipulation tasks demonstrate that Contact Flow enables transfer between human demonstrations and different robotic embodiments.

</details>


### [98] [Enfold: Folding World-Generator Computation into Predictive Representations for Efficient Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26657)
*Weili Zeng,Yitong Xing,Fulong Liu,Chengqun Yang,Antao Xiang,Feng Tian,Jingnan Gao,Jisong Cai,Xin Wang,Xiaomin Wu,Yao Mu,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Enfold方法，将从世界生成模型（如视频预测器）的中间计算状态中提取的多层次表示，迁移到纯当前观测编码器中，从而在部署时无需执行昂贵的生成模型即可进行动作预测，大幅降低推理延迟（最高10.1倍），同时保持强控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界生成模型虽能生成未来轨迹，但每次推理都需执行完整生成过程，计算成本高。本文认为生成过程中的中间计算状态包含丰富的结构化信息（外观、空间布局、交互等），若能将其"内化"到仅依赖当前输入的表示中，则可复用这些计算成果，避免重复生成。

Method: 训练时，利用未来观测作为条件，让生成器（如扩散模型）从被破坏的未来逐步生成连贯轨迹，并暴露其多级中间状态；一个仅依赖当前视觉上下文和语言指令的编码器学习预测这些状态。学到的表示既反馈给生成器以条件化未来生成，也被任务头用于动作预测（任务梯度不反向传播到编码器）。部署时仅使用编码器+任务头，完全跳过生成器。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0和真实机器人任务上，Enfold在保持强控制力的同时，动作预测延迟相比Fast-WAM降低3.7倍，Enfold-Flash版本达到10.1倍加速。表示分析表明，学到的表示能抑制无关变异、优先捕获长期时序变化。在人为干预改变当前场景时，生成器和动作均自适应调整，而非固定轨迹回放。

Conclusion: 世界生成器的核心复用价值不仅是其生成的未来内容，更是构建未来的计算过程本身。通过将生成器的内部结构"折叠"到当前表示中，可在不物化未来的情况下实现高效预测控制，重新定义了生成模型在机器人控制中的角色。

Abstract: World generative models are typically used through what they produce: a rendered future, a video-conditioned action, or latent context computed by a costly generative branch. We argue that their more reusable asset is the computation that constructs a future. As a generator transforms a corrupted future into a coherent trajectory, its intermediate states organize appearance, spatial layout, and interaction across levels of abstraction. Can this future-generative computation be internalized in a representation inferred from the present alone? We present Enfold, which transfers this computation into a representation predicted from the current visual context and language instruction. During training, multi-level states exposed as the generator processes the observed future supervise a current-only encoder. The learned representation is fed back to condition future generation and is read by task heads without allowing task gradients to reshape the encoder. At deployment, action prediction no longer executes the generator. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-robot tasks, Enfold supports strong control while reducing action latency by $3.7\times$ relative to Fast--WAM, Enfold-Flash reaches $10.1\times$. Representation analyses show that it suppresses nuisance variation and preferentially captures changes that emerge over longer horizons. When the current scene is altered by human intervention, both the generated continuation and the executed actions adapt, which is inconsistent with fixed trajectory replay. These results recast a world generator as a source of predictive control representations: its future need not be materialized at every step if its internal structure can be enfolded into the present.

</details>


### [99] [ActSWM: Action-Sensitive World Models for Long-Horizon Planning in Open-World Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26712)
*Zhenfeng Gan,ZiTong Zeng,Jiajun Cheng,Yeke Song,Yongyi Tang,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActSWM，一种基于过渡分离原则的动作敏感潜世界模型，通过约束潜空间 rollout 保持动作依赖差异，解决自回归潜预测器中因上下文崩溃导致的不同动作序列产生不可区分未来状态的问题，在Minecraft等长时交互任务中显著提升了规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜世界模型在长时域展开时存在上下文崩溃问题——不同动作序列产生几乎不可区分的未来潜状态，导致模型预测控制失效。作者旨在建立一种动作敏感的世界模型，使潜动力学能维持动作间的可区分性。

Method: 提出过渡分离原则：①不同动作的未来状态应保持可区分；②每个局部过渡中动作应可恢复。基于此设计ActSWM，将动作敏感性作为潜 rollout 的约束而非仅作为辅助预测目标，以此强制预测未来保留动作依赖差异。

Result: 在阶跃偏移分析、闭环Minecraft规划和跨游戏局部动作恢复三项实验中，ActSWM相比现有基线均保持更大的动作依赖 rollout 差距，提升了长时交互任务成功率，并支持从离线游戏视频中恢复基于世界模型的动作。

Conclusion: 将动作敏感性作为潜动力学模型的显式约束，能有效缓解上下文崩溃，显著提升潜世界模型在长期规划中的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent world models support efficient model-predictive control by optimizing future control sequences in latent space and replanning in a receding-horizon manner. However, existing latent predictors often lack stable long-horizon rollout ability, and prediction accuracy alone does not ensure that rollouts remain responsive to the actions being planned. We identify Context Collapse, a failure mode in which autoregressive latent predictors maintain high similarity to future states while producing nearly indistinguishable futures under different action sequences. To address this issue, we propose ActSWM, an action-sensitive latent world model grounded in a transition-separation principle: a planning-useful latent dynamics model should keep alternative-action futures distinguishable and make the action associated with each local transition recoverable. Under this principle, action sensitivity is enforced as a constraint on latent rollouts rather than treated only as an auxiliary prediction target, encouraging predicted futures to preserve action-dependent differences over long horizons. Across step-drift analysis, closed-loop Minecraft planning, and cross-game local action recovery, ActSWM preserves larger action-dependent rollout gaps than existing baselines, improves task success in long-horizon interactive settings, and enables world-model-based action recovery from offline gameplay videos.

</details>


### [100] [Vision-TL-Action: Neuro-Symbolic Trajectory Generation from Visual Observations and Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26770)
*Zezhi Liu,Zhiwei Zheng,Hanqian Luo,Deyun Qin,Shizhen Wu,Yongchun Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-TL-Action，一种从多视图图像、无坐标TL语法图和机器人初始状态直接生成动作轨迹的方法，无需推理时物体几何信息，通过双向交叉注意力融合TL节点与视觉特征，并采用训练时的谓词-区域对齐目标增强语义接地，在Panda和AntMaze任务上超过或接近全知状态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TL条件轨迹生成器需要编码精确物体几何信息才能实现感知到符号的绑定，限制了模型在真实场景中的泛化能力，本文旨在消除这一依赖，实现从视觉观测直接到结构化TL任务的动作映射。

Method: 1) 输入多视图图像、无坐标TL语法图和机器人初始状态；2) TL节点token与空间视觉token通过双向交叉注意力融合；3) 视觉token仅附加归一化图像平面坐标和相机ID；4) 训练时引入谓词-区域（predicate-to-region）目标以促进对引用对象的视觉接地；5) 使用流匹配（flow-matching）轨迹生成器输出动作轨迹。

Result: Panda任务：Success@1024达到67.45%（oracle基线59.11%）；AntMaze任务：Success@256达96.35%（oracle基线96.88%）。消融实验表明接地细节依赖语义接地质量，谓词身份同时影响注意力和性能。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了从视觉观测和结构化TL目标到动作轨迹的直接映射是可行的，推理时无需物体几何信息，代码已开源。

Abstract: Temporal logic (TL) provides a compositional language for the formulation of long horizon robotic tasks, but existing TL-conditioned trajectory generators can sidestep perception-to-symbol binding by encoding exact object geometry in the task graph. We introduce \emph{Vision-TL-Action}, which generates action trajectories from multi-view images, a coordinate-free TL syntax graph, and the robot initial state. TL-node tokens and spatial visual tokens are fused through bidirectional cross-attention, and the resulting representation conditions a flow-matching trajectory generator. Visual tokens are augmented only with normalized image-plane locations and camera-view identifiers, while a training-only predicate-to-region objective encourages grounding to referenced objects. Consistent with prior work in this domain, we evaluate the model using Success@$K$, the fraction of tasks for which at least one of K sampled trajectories satisfies the TL specification. On Panda task, our model achieves 67.45% Success@1024, compared with 59.11% for the oracle-state baseline. On AntMaze task, it achieves 96.35% Success@256, comparable to the oracle result of 96.88%. Resolution and intervention studies show that spatial detail depends on semantic grounding and predicate identity affects both attention and performance. These results demonstrate a direct mapping from visual observations and structured TL goals to action trajectories without requiring object geometry at inference. Code is available at https://github.com/AricLau07/vision-tl-action.

</details>


### [101] [CheckVLA: Execution-Time Verification with Action-Conditioned World Model for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26789)
*Yushan Liu,Peibo Sun,Xintao Chao,Zhenyang Yang,Yifan Xie,Lingfeng Zhang,Shoujie Li,Chenyu Tang,Fang Chen,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CheckVLA通过动作条件世界模型验证执行，在开放动作块执行过程中恢复反馈，相比周期性重规划在RoboCasa365上提升8.5%成功率，动作条件验证的及时召回率达77.9%远超观测仅控制的48.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常通过开环动作块执行长时域移动操作，但提交后无法响应意外偏差，现有观测异常分数缺乏动作条件参考，无法区分预期效果与意外变化。

Method: 提出CheckVLA，使用独立训练且冻结的动作条件世界模型验证执行。采用保形校准风险阈值决定何时干预，延迟感知硬前缀限制替换为可执行动作，事件驱动关键帧库保留修复前的进度证据。

Result: 在RoboCasa365上，CheckVLA在匹配调用预算下平均成功率36.1%优于周期性重规划的27.6%（+8.5点）。在5%误报目标下，动作条件验证及时召回率77.9%，远超观测仅控制的48.6%和动作打乱控制的37.9%。

Conclusion: 模拟结果支持动作条件验证作为分块执行期间恢复反馈的有效方式，同时保持修复与推理延迟的一致性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies commonly execute long-horizon mobile manipulation through open-loop action chunks, issuing multiple actions without receiving new high-level visual input. A committed chunk therefore implies how observations should evolve, but accidental deviations can violate this expectation while the remaining actions continue to propagate the error: commit-time policy confidence cannot react to a deviation that occurs after dispatch, and observation-only anomaly scores lack an action-conditioned reference for separating expected effects from unexplained changes. We propose CheckVLA, which verifies execution with a separately trained, frozen action-conditioned world model. A conformally calibrated risk threshold bounds the episode-level probability of an unnecessary first intervention and determines when to intervene, its exceedance controls how strongly the rewritten suffix retains the superseded chunk, latency-aware hard prefixing restricts replacement to actions that remain deployable, and an event-driven keyframe bank preserves evidence of prior progress across repairs. On RoboCasa365, under a common training recipe and a matched invocation budget, CheckVLA attains a 36.1% average success rate against 27.6% for periodic replanning (+8.5 points). At a matched 5% episode-level false-alarm target, action conditioning raises timely recall to 77.9%, against 48.6% for an observation-only control and 37.9% for an action-shuffled control. These simulation results support action-conditioned verification as a way to restore feedback during chunked execution while keeping the repair consistent with inference latency.

</details>


### [102] [Risk-Aware Motion Planning with Learned Trajectory Primitives and Probabilistic Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26802)
*Marc Kaufeld,Dian Zhuang,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于径向基函数网络（RBFN）的运动规划框架，用于城市自动驾驶。该方法结合RBFN生成的候选轨迹、碰撞概率评估和优化轨迹细化，在保证安全性和可解释性的同时降低求解复杂度，实验表明相比基准方法具有更好的风险感知能力和更少的车辆极限违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市自动驾驶中，优化-based运动规划虽然精确但计算复杂度高，而纯学习方法缺乏可解释性和安全性保证。现有方法难以在效率、安全性和可解释性之间取得平衡。

Method: 1) 使用RBFN学习 jerk-minimal 轨迹，生成动态一致的候选运动基元；2) 基于解析碰撞概率评估进行候选轨迹筛选；3) 结合优化-based轨迹细化（MPC），在缩减的搜索空间中求解，降低复杂度。

Result: 在多种城市驾驶场景中，该方法相比基准方法表现出更好的风险感知能力，并减少了车辆极限违规次数，验证了安全性和有效性。

Conclusion: 将学习-based轨迹生成与优化-based运动规划相结合，能够在降低求解复杂度的同时保证安全性和可解释性，是一种有效的城市自动驾驶运动规划方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a radial basis function network (RBFN)-informed motion planning framework for safe and efficient urban autonomous driving. The proposed approach combines RBFN-based candidate trajectory generation with an analytic collision probability assessment and optimization-based trajectory refinement. The network learns jerk-minimal trajectories, enabling the MPC to operate within a reduced and dynamically consistent search space. Candidate motion primitives are selected based on an accurate probabilistic risk measure. This design decreases solver complexity while preserving safety and constraint satisfaction. The framework is evaluated in numerous urban driving scenarios. Results demonstrate improved risk awareness and fewer vehicle-limit violations compared to benchmark methods. The proposed approach integrates learning-based trajectories into optimization-based motion planning, thereby ensuring safety and interpretability.

</details>


### [103] [Route by Kinematics, Act by Observation: Kinematics-Supervised Expert Routing in MoE-Augmented VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26807)
*Tianhang Yang,Yanze Zheng,Junjie Wang,Wei-Bin Kou,Ruotong Li,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对MoE-VLA中路由因动作运动学异质性而低效的问题，本文提出KinRT，通过运动学聚类将动作轨迹分组，用组ID监督路由训练，实现从隐式观察驱动路由到显式运动学引导调度的范式转变，在多个基准上提升超20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE增强了VLA的专家专业化能力，但路由模块因操纵任务的运动学异质性以及推理时无法获取运动学信号，导致专家路由效率低下。

Method: 提出Kinematics-supervised explicit routing (KinRT)范式。首先对动作轨迹进行运动学聚类，形成运动学一致的组；用聚类得到的组ID作为真实标签监督路由器训练；推理时路由器仅基于视觉-语言观察进行专家调度，无需动作运动学信息。这本质上是一种非对称桥接机制，在训练时将任务运动学从动作空间蒸馏到观察空间。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准上提升超过23.26%，在自建DIYRobot平台上提升20.27%，优于密集模型和MoE-featured VLA模型。

Conclusion: KinRT通过运动学监督的显式路由，有效解决了MoE-VLA中路由低效问题，且具有良好的跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: While MoE augments VLA via expert specialization, router suffers from ineffective expert routing owing to the kinematic heterogeneity of actions across manipulation tasks and, even worse, the unavailability of the kinematic signals at inference time. In this work, we first observe that most semantically distinct manipulation tasks reduce to multiple kinematic archetypes. Motivated by this finding, we propose Kinematics-supervised explicit routing (KinRT), a new paradigm that shifts from implicit, observation-driven expert routing to explicit, kinematics-guided expert dispatching. Specifically, we perform kinematic clustering on action trajectories into multiple kinematically coherent groups, whose IDs serve as ground truth to supervise the training of the router; at inference time, the router dispatches experts only using visual-language observations, without any reliance on action kinematics. KinRT actually introduces an asymmetric bridging mechanism that distills the task kinematics from the action space in training into the observation space at inference. In addition, to assess KinRT's cross-platform generalization, we build an economical, Do-It-Yourself robot (DIYRobot) platform from scratch using 3D-print technology ($<$ 2,000USD). Extensive experiments demonstrate KinRT's superiority over both dense and MoE-featured VLAs by more than 23.26% on RoboTwin benchmark and 20.27% on our introduced DIYRobot platform. Our code and DIYRobot platform will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [104] [Practice Makes Policies: Bootstrapping and Consolidating Robotic Capabilities from Zero Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26809)
*Jialiang Li,Yuhan Wang,Haojun Li,Gaojing Zhang,Yangtian Ye,Qipeng Liu,Haotian Liang,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HERO，一种自改进的分层具身智能体，无需人类示教即可自主进化操作能力，通过组织启发式推理、示例复用和反射执行，实现从零到高效闭环视觉运动策略的渐进式能力进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作系统以静态方式获取技能，缺乏通过物理交互自适应进化的机制，无法像人类通过反复练习形成肌肉记忆那样持续提升操作能力。

Method: 提出HERO框架，将启发式推理、示例复用和反射执行组织为统一编排框架，通过自主引导操作经验、经验迁移快速积累可复用行为、渐进巩固重复交互为闭环视觉运动策略，并将自主数据收集与任务执行紧密结合。

Result: 大量实验表明HERO显著减少了机器人数据收集过程中的人为干预，同时在多样化任务中实现了稳健的操作能力。

Conclusion: HERO提供了一条通向自改进机器人系统的有前景路径，实现了无需人类示教的能力自主进化。

Abstract: General-purpose robotic manipulation requires robots to perform diverse tasks in open-world environments while improving their skills over time. Despite recent progress in robotic manipulation, existing systems still primarily acquire manipulation skills in a static manner, where capabilities are learned for specific tasks or settings rather than adaptively evolving through physical interaction. Resembling how repeated practice enables humans to develop muscle memory, advanced manipulation proficiency requires an autonomous capability evolution mechanism that allows robots to progressively transform interaction experiences into increasingly effective manipulation abilities. To this end, we propose HERO, a self-improving hierarchical embodied agent that enables autonomous capability evolution from zero human demonstrations. HERO organizes heuristic reasoning, exemplar reuse, and reflexive execution into a unified orchestration framework, allowing robots to autonomously bootstrap manipulation experience, rapidly accumulate reusable behaviors through experience transfer, and progressively consolidate recurring interactions into efficient closed-loop visuomotor policies. By tightly coupling autonomous data collection with task execution, HERO continuously expands and dynamically schedules manipulation capabilities according to different stages of experience accumulation and execution requirements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HERO substantially reduces human intervention during robotic data collection while achieving robust manipulation across diverse tasks, providing a promising path toward self-improving robotic systems.

</details>


### [105] [From Uncertainty to Determinism: Coarse-to-Fine Visual Floorplan Localization without Ray Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26817)
*Shiyong Meng,Bolei Chen,Ping Zhong,Yang Wan,Rongzhi Wang,Jiazhi Xia,Jianxin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从不确定性到确定性的粗到细视觉地板定位框架(FLoc)，通过图像条件姿态扩散模型进行多模态姿态分布参数化，并结合局部精化器实现亚米级定位精度，无需离线地图预处理或测试时查找表，在S3D和ZInD基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉地板定位(FLoc)面临跨模态信息不对称和重复室内布局导致的多模态姿态分布问题，现有基于射线匹配的方法存在信息损失、预处理开销大和推理时穷举匹配效率低等不足。

Method: 提出粗到细框架：粗阶段使用图像条件姿态扩散模型对连续多模态姿态分布进行参数化，将随机初始化的姿态粒子路由到不同候选模式；细阶段设计局部精化器，从以候选为中心的平面图裁剪块中预测亚米级姿态残差。

Result: 在S3D（完整版）和ZInD基准测试上，该方法在准确性和鲁棒性方面均达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡了全局多假设跟踪与局部亚米级精化，无需任何离线地图预处理或测试时查找表，克服了传统射线匹配范式的局限性。

Abstract: Visual Floorplan Localization (FLoc) has emerged as a promising solution for indoor localization by matching egocentric images against minimalist structural maps.
